下载

包类型 平台 说明 文件
一键安装包（推荐） Windows 7+ x64 内嵌完整运行环境，支持一键安装、自动登录与配置 QQChatExporter-Installer-v6.4.1.exe
Shell 模式 Windows x64 独立无头 (Headless) 运行环境，适合服务器或自动化部署 NapCat-QCE-Windows-x64-v6.4.1.zip
Shell 模式 Linux x64 独立无头 (Headless) 运行环境，适合服务器或 Docker 部署 NapCat-QCE-Linux-x64-v6.4.1.tar.gz
Shell 模式（预览） macOS Apple Silicon (arm64) 独立运行环境，首次启动会在本地生成一份专用的 QQ 运行副本，详见 macOS 使用指南 NapCat-QCE-macOS-arm64-v6.4.1.tar.gz
Framework 模式 Windows 作为 QQNT 插件运行，与桌面端 QQ 共存 NapCat-Framework-QCE-v6.4.1.zip
NapCat 插件商店包 跨平台 供已有 NapCat 环境的用户直接在插件商店安装 napcat-plugin-qce.zip

版本选择指南

  • 一键安装包：Windows 用户的首选。双击即可自动完成环境配置与登录（支持快捷登录或扫码），安装完毕后将自动唤起 WebUI 面板。
  • Shell 模式：独立运行版本，需单独进行 QQ 登录，适用于服务器环境部署及自动化备份需求。macOS 版本目前为预览支持，仅适用于 Apple Silicon（arm64）。
  • Framework 模式：作为 QQNT 插件运行，与当前使用的桌面端 QQ 共享登录状态，适合日常使用 Windows 桌面版 QQ 的用户。
  • NapCat 插件商店包：适用于已部署 NapCat 环境（含本地安装、Docker 或宿主机部署）的用户。请直接在 NapCat WebUI 的插件商店中搜索安装，无需手动下载此压缩包。

<details> <summary>Shell 模式使用方法</summary>

完整模式（需登录 QQ，支持导出新增聊天记录）：

  • Windows：解压后双击运行 launcher-user.bat
  • Linux：终端运行 ./launcher-user.sh
  • macOS：终端运行 ./launcher-user.sh，详见 macOS 使用指南

独立模式（无需登录 QQ，仅用于浏览已导出的记录文件）：

  • Windows：双击运行 start-standalone.bat
  • Linux：终端运行 ./start-standalone.sh
  • macOS：终端运行 ./start-standalone.sh

启动后，请在浏览器中访问 http://localhost:40653/qce。若使用完整模式，需输入控制台输出的访问令牌 (Access Token) 进行验证。

</details>

<details> <summary>Framework 模式使用方法</summary>

方式 A：通过 napiLoader.bat 运行（推荐）

此方式独立运行，无需预先安装 LiteLoaderQQNT。

  1. 解压下载的 NapCat-Framework-QCE-v6.4.1.zip 文件
  2. 确保当前已彻底退出正在运行的 QQ 客户端
  3. 双击运行 napiLoader.bat 脚本
  4. 若弹出 QQ 登录界面，请按常规方式完成登录
  5. 在浏览器中访问 http://localhost:40653/qce

方式 B：通过 LiteLoaderQQNT 加载（仅限特定需求用户）

此方式要求系统已配置好 LiteLoaderQQNT 环境。

  1. 参照 LiteLoaderQQNT 官方文档 完成前置框架的安装
  2. 打开 QQ 设置，确认左侧菜单栏已出现 LiteLoaderQQNT 选项
  3. 按照 LiteLoaderQQNT 或 NapCat 官方文档中的标准插件安装流程，将本插件部署至对应目录

<details> <summary>如何找到访问令牌？</summary>

按下 Win + R 键，输入 %USERPROFILE%\.qq-chat-exporter 并回车。打开该目录下的 security.json 文件，查找 accessToken 字段的值即可：

{
  "accessToken": "此处即为你的访问令牌",
  ...
}

</details>

</details>

<details> <summary>系统要求</summary>

需配合 QQ 客户端版本 34606 及以上使用（推荐版本 9.9.19-34740），前往下载。

  • Windows：支持 Windows 10/11 x64。如运行报错提示缺少 DLL 文件，请安装 Visual C++ 运行库。
  • Linux：支持 Ubuntu 20.04+ / Debian 11+ / CentOS 8+。内置 使用静态 musl 构建，不要求特定 glibc 版本；QQ、NapCat 和注入模块仍需满足各自的系统依赖。
  • macOS：⚠️ 预览支持，仅限 Apple Silicon（M1 及以上，arm64）。需已安装官方 macOS 版 QQ 与 Xcode 命令行工具（xcode-select --install，用于 codesign）。首次启动会在本地生成并签名一份专用的 QQ 运行副本，不会修改 /Applications/QQ.app 本体；该副本会失去系统 App 沙盒保护，仅用于后台运行 NapCat，详见 macOS 使用指南。Intel（x64）Mac 暂未提供官方安装包。

</details>

[!CAUTION] 本项目完全免费且开源，遵循 GPL-3.0 开源协议。如果您是通过付费渠道获取的本软件，请立即申请退款并进行举报。

Yeachan-Heo/oh-my-codex ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.21.47 Stable2026-11-07T14:25:54Z

oh-my-codex v0.21.47

Summary

This patch release keeps psmux worker panes alive on native Windows by embedding the split receipt safely in tmux commands for all platforms.

Fixed

  • PSMUX WINDOWS WORKER LAUNCH (#4008, fixes #4007): On native Windows with psmux, omx team workers exited at launch because OMX appended a # omx_source_... receipt to the worker command. psmux does not treat # as a comment starter, so the receipt reached powershell.exe as extra arguments. The receipt is now appended as a final '; # <receipt>' statement inside the PowerShell script before -EncodedCommand encoding; POSIX keeps ' # <receipt>' inside the sh -c string; and paneHasReceipt decodes encoded commands for ambiguous-split reconciliation.

Compatibility

No breaking API changes. Release-coupled metadata was synchronized to 0.21.47 on dev by #4003, so the release branch does not bump those values again. package.json, both root package-lock.json version entries, the Cargo workspace and six workspace crates, and plugin metadata all report 0.21.47 at the pinned source.

Verification

  • npm ci — passed; 172 packages installed and lifecycle hooks completed.
  • npm run build — passed.
  • npm test — passed on the subsequent full run: runtime build, all 475 Node test files, generated catalog check, and prompt-inventory check completed.
  • npm run check:no-unused — passed.
  • npm run lint — passed across 900 files. Biome emitted the existing linter.rules.recommended deprecation notice; no lint fixes were applied.
  • Focused native-config regression tests — passed: 36 passed, 0 failed.
  • Focused codex-hooks.test.js regression suite — passed: 74 passed, 1 skipped, 0 failed.

Contributors

Thanks to @Yeachan-Heo for the psmux receipt embedding fix (#4008) and to @aglikman for reporting the issue (#4007).

Full Changelog: v0.21.46...v0.21.47

— [repo owner's gaebal-gajae (clawdbot) 🦞]

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v5.1.63 Stable2026-11-07T12:38:10Z

The macOS keychain helper's lookup paths and tests are correct on Windows CI. The macOS install locations (the OmO.app runtime and helper copies) are built with POSIX separators on every host, and the #11262 keychain-helper tests use platform-correct paths, so dev's Windows test legs pass again. (#12690, part of #11262)

Fixed: the first device listing no longer waits about 10 seconds for each signed-out or otherwise unsupported device; the bunshin SDK answers such a device's tool list as empty at once instead of trying to describe it. (#12680)

Internal: the vendored bunshin SDK imports its own pinned typebox through omo-senpi's #bunshin-ts-dep/ imports instead of the bare package name, so senpi's extension registry can no longer hand it a different typebox; no user-visible change. (#12676) (#12680)

Thread ops on another device spend fewer relay round trips: the session's attach is kept across ops, and the session's model catalog is held on it, so a model switch to an exact provider/id that catalog lists is one set_model. A set that may have run is still reported as idempotency_uncertain; a refused one re-reads the catalog and answers as a fresh catalog would. Status and transcript reads (get_state, get_messages) still go to the device every time, because the engine's in-memory state changes without a record on the attach. (#12672)

Starting omo no longer reinstalls and restarts a running device service that is only slow to answer: on macOS and Linux a handshake timeout is retried once and then reported as degraded, instead of being treated as a stopped service. (#12665)

A message sent to a thread on another device whose engine answers started without a turn id (senpi 2026.11.2) is now confirmed with the running turn that get_state reports, instead of idempotency_uncertain. (#12660)

Fixed: closing a thread on another device answers closed instead of uncertain (reply_lost); the close was applied there, but its answer was dropped when the other device ended the session's attachment. (#12655)

Fixed: a thread created on another device (thread_create with a device, or a fork there) is named by the id that device lists it by, so reading, messaging, interrupting, switching the model of and closing it work instead of answering session_not_found. (#12651)

Internal: knip ignores the closed-device-login preload fixture (a --preload test file, nothing imports it); no behavior change. (#12648)

A delegated child whose own model chain has no connected provider now fails with one message that names every model in that chain and the exact fix, instead of an opaque "no available model". The chain is listed in order with the providers each model needs, the refusal says which providers are not connected, and it names the key the app's Settings editor writes (categories.<name>.model for a category, agents.<name>.model for an agent), so the parent session can act on the first line instead of retrying a dead binding. The same refusal now covers a lane pinned to a model this machine's registry does not serve, naming the pin and the Settings key. Nothing is substituted and no child is spawned (#12634). (#12636)

Internal (tests only): in the omo-native trust-stage TLS probe, a channel that hangs now reports "timeout" instead of false, so a hang can no longer pass an assertion that expects a TLS rejection. (#12629)

The skills audit's third pass corrects three search commands and one retired category name in the shipped skills (#12525).

  • Three rg examples used rg -iE. In ripgrep -E is --encoding, not a pattern-type flag, so the combined form consumes the next argument and the examples did not do what they claimed; they now use rg -i.
  • One refactor guidance line told an agent to fall back from deep; that category was split into deep-low / deep-high, so it now names deep-high. (#12615)

The refactor-squad member prompts keep team_send_message + team_task_update. The team-mode surface does not expose task_send to members, so an earlier draft of this change that rewrote those prompts to task_send({ to: "lead" }) was wrong and has been reverted here. (#12615)

The skills audit's second pass fixed 24 more places where shipped text disagreed with the code. The 591-row community audit left 213 rows "partial"; re-reading them against the current sources, plus the 83 reference rows an earlier pass rejected, found these real ones (#12525). ast-grep's examples now put the search path before --lang (the helper rejects it after, on the Python 3.9 that stock macOS ships) and describe $$$ as the lazy matcher ast-grep 0.51 actually is. refactor's ast-grep call sites now say the helper lives in the ast-grep skill, and mass-ulw's graph example uses new Bun.Glob(...) instead of a glob() the eval kernel does not have. Contradictions inside one surface are gone too: the refactor trigger threshold, browser's WAF routing, ulw-loop's different-goal and self-execution rules, ulw-plan's review-round count and git category, ulw-execute's next-wave amend, mass-ulw's SDK surface and delivery-gate list, ulw-research's sum context fields, visual-qa's 1920 captures, debugging's post-cleanup evidence line, the accent-on-two-primaries line, and git-master's commit gate versus the workflows that mandate commits. (#12614)

Shipped skills no longer give agents contradictory instructions or describe commands that do not work. A community audit of the installed skills listed 591 places where two skills, or a skill and the code, disagree; two model families confirmed 118 root causes in this repo, and 117 are fixed (#12525). The ultrawork verification gate now spawns omo-native-gate-reviewer instead of plan-reviewer, which only reviews plan files. ulw-execute --make-pr hands off with the PR URL and merges only when asked, while --ship merges. ultrawork, review-work, ulw-execute and ulw-loop now agree on who runs final QA. Skills now use the real task_send({ to }) shape, codexGoalJson, session-scoped .omo/ulw-loop/<session-id>/ paths, the lsp_* tool names and LSP config paths each harness reads, ast-grep commands and rule shapes that ast-grep accepts, and the dag SDK's result envelope. The 250-line split rule, the no-refactor rules for bugfixes and legacy code, the Miri ship gate, the SIZE_OK and catch opt-out markers, the ulw-research gate order, and the visual-qa spacing and motion checks no longer contradict each other. (#12528)

Computer use is ready for the tool side of a split session: there the audit file goes to the call owner's audit directory on the device, audit records carry the calling session's id, and capture sizing follows the policy sent with the call instead of a model the device does not have; local sessions behave exactly as before. (#12452)

omo-senpi sessions on macOS no longer wake for every file write in HOME. The config watch added a "a .omo may appear here" target for the cwd and every parent up to and including HOME, and a directory watch on macOS receives every change beneath it, so each session handled every write in HOME. Those creation targets now stop at the project root (the first parent holding .git), and never include the user config directory's parent (HOME, which the user-config target already covers), any directory that contains it, or the filesystem root. .omo directories that already exist are still watched up to HOME; a brand-new .omo above the project root is picked up at the next session start. (#12397)

omo remote attach forwards every engine command to the remote session and shows the engine's own answer. The attach view no longer keeps its own list of allowed commands: apart from its handshake, every record the attach TUI sends goes unchanged to the remote device, whose engine decides it. The engine's reply comes back on the same request with its data (a large reply is reassembled and checked first), refusals are shown exactly as the remote side states them, and dialogs and events the engine sends for those commands reach the TUI. The device-service client speaks the current local API: one engine record per command, the delegated command channel, session.endpoints, listing rows with kind and parent, and the newer refusal and detach reasons. (#12261)

Desktop memory sync now uses the desktop's account instead of the CLI's. An engine the desktop launches claims its private memory-sync channel at startup, answers with the bootstrap handshake and resolves the sync account through the desktop. A launch without that channel still uses the account omo login keeps, and a declared channel that fails reports memory sync as unavailable rather than falling back to CLI credentials. Windows keeps refusing the channel until its pipe-pair path lands. (#12008) (#12064)

Desktop memory sync now attaches on Windows. The engine's receiver no longer refuses win32: it uses the stream senpi hands over for the declared memory-sync channel, which senpi builds from two one-way pipes with their inherit flag already cleared, so the omo side opens no descriptor and spawns nothing. A declared channel that senpi did not adopt still reports memory sync as unavailable, never falling back to CLI credentials. This replaces the Windows refusal that #12008 noted. (#12025) (#12064)

macOS sign-in gets the client side of its signed keychain owner. New modules check the keychain helper's Developer ID signature (an unsigned or ad-hoc copy is never run), find a verified copy, talk to it under the session lock, and keep one approval per process; sign-in uses them through the keychain store below. (#11586, part of #11262) (#11613)

Session locks can be handed to a child process for one call. withLock now gives its callback a lease: handOver(pid) makes a child the lock's live holder and restore() takes it back, aborting the critical section if the lock changed meanwhile. The macOS keychain helper uses it so no other writer can enter while a keychain change is landing. (#11578, part of #11262) (#11613)

macOS sign-in stops asking for keychain access again and again. With OmO's signed keychain helper installed, the sign-in item moves to the helper the first time a token is used, after which reads need no dialog; an item another app created asks at most once per machine with a plain dialog, never a password dialog. Windows and Linux are unchanged. (#11589, part of #11262) (#11613)

omo doctor checks the macOS keychain helper. It reports a missing or damaged helper as a warning (sign-in keeps working through the previous path), and an item the OmO desktop app created is now moved to the helper by the app itself before the CLI would ask for keychain access. (#11613, part of #11262)

Changed

  • thread_send and thread_handoff to a session on another device no longer read the session's state before the prompt: the prompt goes out first as a follow-up, and the other device's engine decides whether it starts a turn or waits behind the running one. On today's engines a send takes two sequential relay round trips instead of three: the prompt, then one state read for the started turn's id (idle session) or for the queue position (busy session). A send that starts a turn drops to one round trip once the other device's engine carries senpi#6135 (which fixes senpi#5999). A send that arrives while the other session has claimed a turn but not yet started it now answers queued instead of failing as unsupported. A steer still reads the session's state first. (#12679)
  • The visualize, data-scientist and frontend skills now teach how to build a chart page shown inline beside a reply. Figures are computed at their source (SQL in the database the data lives in, DuckDB or Polars over files). An inline chart is drawn in HTML instead of a matplotlib PNG, with :root { color-scheme: light dark; } so light-dark() fallbacks follow the theme. Bars are HTML elements in a fixed-height container, the first and last axis labels are aligned to the chart edges so neither clips at 390 px, days are cut in the reader's time zone with a partial day marked on its own bar, and one figure is cross-checked against a second source. The preview step now measures each chart's largest bar and legend colours in dark and light at the reply width and 390 px. frontend exempts an inline page from its DESIGN.md gate, Lighthouse audit and research lanes, which visualize already assumed. (#12612)
  • Internal: the memory component's launch classification is one shared function, so the compiled-executable desktop-sync test exercises the same check omo.js runs. No behavior change. (#12053) (#12064)
  • Internal: a test now guards that the memory component's shared launch classification module imports nothing, so omo.js's registration half cannot pick up sidecar or private-fds code through it. No behavior change. (#12064)

Fixed

  • A thread create or fork whose endpoint lookup on the other device times out or loses its connection is now reported as refused, since nothing was sent, instead of uncertain. A timeout after the command was sent stays uncertain. (#12650, part of #12320)
  • omo login --device and omo login --no-browser now sign in while OmO accounts are still closed, so a computer with no browser or desktop app (for example a server you reach over SSH) can sign in by approving a code on another device. Plain omo login, omo logout and omo whoami still point you to the desktop app. (#12640)
  • A published omo build can no longer ship the all-zero device-service release key placeholder: the service-auth release step now refuses a key set that is not a strict ed25519 set, and a build with no usable key reports the build defect instead of telling a current user to update. (#12602)
  • A received memory-sync key naming a path that exists as a FIFO (or another non-regular file) no longer hangs the cycle: the leaf is refused with a receipt before it is read, so the rest of the batch still applies. (#12568, refs #12158)
  • The memory-core exec-timeout diagnostics case no longer fails under host load: the record's process table is asserted by contract (a table naming the timed-out git, or the process snapshot failed: <reason> form when the snapshot itself times out) instead of requiring the table (#12145). (#12545)
  • The onboarding context no longer embeds the absolute filesystem path of the installed built-in skills root, so the session context is identical for identical builds whatever the install location. The tour now names the built-in onboarding skill, which the skills listing resolves by name (#12150). (#12544)
  • When a user or project skill named onboarding shadows the built-in in the skills listing, the tour falls back to the built-in skill's absolute path instead of silently following the shadowing skill; the EQ goldens are re-recorded for the new pointer text (#12544 review). (#12544)
  • Contributed skills (computer-use, x-search) again yield to any loaded same-name skill, even when the listing carries no path for it, so no duplicate surfaces as a skill-conflict collision; with onboarding in disabled_skills the tour falls back to the built-in path instead of naming a hidden skill (#12544 review). (#12544)
  • With only your own memory identity on disk, ls, find, grep and glob on ~/.omo, ~/.omo/memory or any other folder above the memory area are no longer refused as "belongs to another memory identity", including when Finder or Explorer left a .DS_Store or desktop.ini there. The guard now refuses a search from above the memory area only while another identity's directory (durable, repo-less, or a live task run) exists there, and the refusal says that one was found without naming it. (#12301) (#12476)

Added

  • Thread tools use this device's device service to reach your other devices, on in every session with no setting; a device without the service keeps today's local behavior. (#12336, part of #12320)

  • Thread tools take a thread's account-wide alias and a device to create a thread on, the groundwork for reaching your other signed-in devices: every call answers exactly as it would on this device, and a Free account gets a clear error. Reaching another device arrives with the device-service wiring that follows. A new thread_close ends a session on this device or on another one for every client that holds it; a thread with no live session answers closed: false, and a terminal's own session stays with its terminal. (#12335, part of #12320)

  • The headless desktop fake backend (senpi-desktop-backend-fake) can script window identity and identity changes, key-priming focus reads and focus-record outcomes, hit reads, a timed user-input history with the frontmost pid, an accessibility deadline and dense-tree knob, epoch events, a display change, and a stop marker. Every new hook records a sink op, every field defaults to today's behavior, and a timeline that could never fire (nth: 0, a press more than an hour away) is rejected at load. (#12313, part of #12232)

  • The desktop engine protocol gains an opt-in action-report vocabulary on protocol 1, with no version or ABI change. engine.hello lists features: ["action-report"], and a session opened with reportMode: "object" gets an ActionReport from every mutating request. The report says whether input reached the target and whether retrying is safe: one of seven {delivery, retry} pairs. Errors from those requests carry the same disposition. Sessions that don't opt in see the same replies as before, apart from the new features list. The protocol also gains the error codes AmbiguousTarget, CaptureConsentRequired and ProtectedProcess, the epoch.changed notification, and optional window-identity and accessibility-evidence fields that later changes will fill in. Frame ids are now unique per engine process, so a frame id from before a session re-open can never match a new capture. (#12260, part of #12234)

  • omo Native eval cells can reach this user's machines through the bunshin TypeScript SDK: the plugin bundles @sisyphuslabs/bunshin-ts and @sisyphuslabs/bunshin-ts-relay (vendored build output pinned per file, not npm dependencies) into runtime/bunshin-ts/sdk.js, publishes its directory as OMO_BUNSHIN_TS_SDK_ROOT at activation, and the installer requires it. pickTransport (BUNSHIN_TS_TRANSPORT = auto, relay or hub) uses the local device service when it reports connected; this build bundles no hub transport. Over the relay, one device that is slow, offline or refused on machine.describe no longer fails connect(), listMachines() or fanout() for every machine: it is listed unsupported with attention naming why and after how long, fanout skips it, and the device the cell runs on is listed too (unsupported, since the service runs no machine operation on its own device). A bunshin skill pointer and removed-tool hint teach the import. (#12258, fixes #12251)

trycua/cua ↗ 17 releases · 7 stable · 10 prerelease
cua-driver-rs-v0.41.3cua-driver-rs: v0.41.3 Prerelease2026-11-07T12:10:03Z

Cua Driver 0.41.3

Summary

This release includes 5 fixes.

Fixes

  • one macOS focus arbiter decides every activation undo. (#6803)
  • report a timed-out capture worker to the next caller. (#6809)
  • commit or roll back browser setup explicitly. (#6808)
  • hold every tool route on runtime admission while permissions are pending. (#6802)
  • admit per-pid state-capture workers in one critical section. (#6807)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

Cua Driver 0.41.3-nightly.20261107.42337971369 (nightly)

Automated immutable nightly built from an exact main commit. Nightlies are opt-in, may be less stable than regular releases, and never replace stable update discovery.

Source

  • Commit: 14ae717630ae3fb12eed3be3da0755d1fbb04f0e
  • Changes: nightly-cua-driver-rs-v0.38.1-nightly.20261105.41967274244…14ae717630ae

Changes

  • Fix: add SPDX headers to cua-driver sources (CUA-3388). (#6516)
  • Fix: treat niri workspace read-backs the cleanup deadline cut off as unconfirmed. (#6520)
  • Fix: keep a full budget for each dom_event click readback. (#6519)
  • Fix: initialize recording hooks for embedded platform registries. (#5785) reported by @nick-inkeep.
  • Fix: keep cua-driver-core clippy-clean without default features. (#6543)
  • Fix: raise own-process windows on the AppKit main queue (CUA-3309). (#6559)
  • Fix: return from a trusted browser click when its handler opens a dialog. (#6562)
  • Fix: Windows type_text posts UTF-16 units in order and repairs dropped tails (CUA-3122). (#6341)
  • Ci: retry apt with backoff and fall back to archive.debian.org when snapshot.debian.org fails. (#6582)
  • Feat: keep typed secrets out of recordings. (#6333) reported by @radimsem.
  • Ci: rebuild_tag dispatch to republish an existing draft tag. (#6606)
  • Fix: gate YAML capability-manifest tests on the yaml feature. (#6627)
  • Ci: let rebuild dispatches pass the macOS hosted gate. (#6613)
  • Fix: derive desktop coordination from the action contract. (#6638)
  • Ci: nothing in trycua/cua depends on GitHub's repo-wide Latest release (CUA-3552). (#6578)
  • Fix: move the skill version under metadata.version. (#6647) Thanks @Mearman.
  • Fix: bound browser_dialog Page.enable retry by the dialog subscription deadline. (#6643)
  • Fix: clear a dialog record Chromium no longer shows. (#6514)
  • Fix: keep the Windows daemon pid file owned by the running daemon. (#6654)
  • Fix: lock the daemon socket so a racing launch cannot orphan a daemon. (#6652)
  • Fix: cover the desktop lane for invoke_menu and type_text_chars on Linux. (#6653)
  • Fix: clear Rust 1.97 clippy lints in platform-linux. (#6658)
  • Fix: find a macOS app by any name it ships, without guessing. (#6657) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • Fix: press a shortcut's key where the keyboard layout types it on macOS. (#6659) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • Fix: refuse clicks fast while a detached session's dialog blocks the tab. (#6646)
  • Ci: remove 26 dead workflows and two unused libs (CUA-3571). (#6616)
  • Fix: publish Cua Driver SDKs when post-publish bookkeeping fails. (#6680)
  • Ci: deprecate cua-agent, cua-core, cua-bench-ui and the xfce, kasm and qemu images (CUA-3571). (#6620)
  • Fix: refuse disabled macOS controls on every token writer. (#6503)
  • Ci: queue auto-merge on installer bump PRs instead of closing them. (#6694)
  • Fix: start the composited X11 overlay test's reads once its desktop is on screen. (#6512)
  • Fix: re-read AXEnabled after foreground activation on macOS. (#6702)
  • Fix: clear platform-linux test-target clippy lints. (#6695)
  • Test: align Hyprland release-contract tests with the rebuild_tag workflow. (#6713)
  • Fix: keep background popups and drags off the user's seat (CUA-3582). (#6705)
  • Fix: admit any native Wayland app to Hyprland background input. (#6706)
  • Ci: label the post-publish installer bump PR no-release. (#6746)
  • Fix: refuse MCP requests before initialize on HTTP and envelope transports. (#6743)
  • Fix: keep polling macOS grants after the permissions gate times out. (#6739)
  • Fix: keep idle-end launch ownership only until a terminal end. (#6738) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • Fix: keep a macOS sheet's own id for focus and snapshot scope. (#6758) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • Fix: restore the prior X11 foreground after foreground type_text. (#6768)
  • Feat: add cua-driver test to check a new install end to end (CUA-3660). (#6765)
  • Fix: keep a session's last use on its lifecycle record. (#6773)
  • Fix: restore the Windows foreground only after typed input drained. (#6779)
  • Fix: finalize the previous recording when a new one starts. (#6794)
  • Fix: one macOS focus arbiter decides every activation undo. (#6803)

Cua Driver 0.41.2

Summary

This release includes 1 fix.

Fixes

  • finalize the previous recording when a new one starts. (#6794)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

cua-driver-rs-v0.41.0cua-driver-rs: v0.41.0 Prerelease2026-11-07T01:00:45Z

Cua Driver 0.41.0

Summary

This release adds 1 features and includes 2 fixes.

Features

  • add cua-driver test to check a new install end to end (CUA-3660). (#6765)

Fixes

  • keep a macOS sheet's own id for focus and snapshot scope. (#6758) Thanks @AlexFalher.
  • restore the prior X11 foreground after foreground type_text. (#6768)

Contributors

Thanks to @AlexFalher for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

cua-driver-rs-v0.40.5cua-driver-rs: v0.40.5 Prerelease2026-11-06T22:06:59Z

Cua Driver 0.40.5

Summary

This release includes 2 fixes.

Fixes

  • refuse MCP requests before initialize on HTTP and envelope transports. (#6743)
  • keep polling macOS grants after the permissions gate times out. (#6739)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

heygen-com/hyperframes ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.9.20 Stable2026-11-07T11:25:12Z

HyperFrames v0.9.20

Released on 2026-11-07.

Cloud export of footage cuts works again, Safari no longer freezes on a video stuck at its header, and any video format nobody listed now gets a playable copy instead of a blank card.

Features

  • Check: Name the saved report file and print one stderr line per check step (9ac2a2387, #7344).
  • CLI: Audio --read --layer reads a one-layer file whole, no separation (b37db1122, #7345).
  • Studio: Type an exact preview zoom and see a pixel grid at high zoom (25a61016d, #5708).
  • Studio: Improve element picker cancellation feedback (f004622a6, #6900).
  • Studio: Show canvas gesture position, size and rotation (a4a84deec, #5594).
  • CLI: Support JSON telemetry status (6c0cab814, #7289).
  • CLI: The sign-in page the browser lands on follows the design (9acdce4a2, #7332).
  • Studio: Show the agent's storyboard in a Storyboard tab beside the preview (318724f5f, #7284).
  • Core: Motion blur reports what a composition holds, as counts (a874a6324, #7306).
  • CLI: Check_report counts the browser-playable copies a check waited for (de2d21a8a, #7316).
  • CLI: Check_report names the finding that stopped the check (9fcd06cbb, #7305).
  • Core: Auto motion blur draws with the filter tier (e2bb7da25, #7288).
  • Share: Extract shared review kernel for local hosts (f54b2227f, #7134).
  • CLI: A line_unread finding carries the frames it measured around where the line was first read (a81b34410, #7294).

Fixes

  • CLI: Cloud upload tests stop flaking and two cuts at the same age count as one (555c788a3, #7352).
  • Core: Skip a video the browser can't decode instead of freezing the film (9058fd1a7, #7355).
  • Studio Server: A video with a codec nobody listed gets a playable preview copy (cf75dfd7a, #7356).
  • CLI: Cloud render zip carries files the composition references under dot-folders (6161ae740, #7353).
  • Studio Server: MPEG-2 and DNxHD videos get a playable preview copy (6004508e6, #7348).
  • CLI: A cloud upload cut at a fixed age stops early and shows its progress (65c62f314, #7350).
  • Check: A Korean number and its counter count as one word in line reading time (bc2773d04, #7343).
  • Core: Words in a scene the preview mounts later keep their spaces (1ddd42558, #7342).
  • Lint: Flag crypto.randomUUID() as non-deterministic (cc7a760f0, #7322).
  • Core: A render no longer rewinds when a set sits beside a tween on another property (539c70cee, #7339).
  • CLI: Price GPT Image 2.6 and every GPT Image reference picture (ab50e9c14, #7307).
  • Engine: A missing frame file skips its self-verify sample instead of aborting the render (cfa0d0cef, #7334).
  • Producer: A failure before the first frame reports frame capture as its stage (fe0f54b38, #7331).
  • Engine: A render no longer finishes without an image that loaded late (a9a8d8937, #7304).
  • Producer: Segmented capture gives up on a session stuck opening or initializing (6fab21914, #7329).
  • Engine: DrawElement falls back on gpu-less linux and beginframe launches instead of hanging self-verify (18fc75dcd, #7324).
  • Core: A set that interrupts a tween holds in a real render at any worker count (3e3ba8d0d, #7318).
  • Producer: Render reads a composition src like the preview, refuses paths outside (4cf26b5b4, #7317).
  • Skills: Reverse hands billing commands to the person instead of running them (410a516ce, #7314).
  • Core: A decoded buffer's handover leaves the routed element silent (cb8e02e82, #7309).
  • Studio Server: Proxy MPEG-4 Part 2 and MJPEG video the browser cannot decode (f3e45b492, #7310).
  • Templates: Pin the five style-sampling packs with the release-check fixes (9e2510bda, #7263).
  • CLI: Media-use voices says whose clones it lists, and --voice takes language codes (fc0b53361, #7261).
  • Core: Video starts a second early and catches up hidden, so the picture meets the cut (3e86def58, #7265).
  • CLI: Cloud render logs a line a minute while a job holds its status (41b7aeeaf, #7262).
  • CLI: Installing Chrome on a busy Windows PC no longer takes ten minutes (8fd754207, #7301).
  • Core: Accented Latin letters draw in a bundled family at its bundled weights (6011d9d0d, #7299).
  • Studio: Pressing Shift no longer re-renders the timeline (66cbabc39, #7298).
  • Core: A mover's ::before and ::after outside its box stay in the blur (4bee04e4a, #7295).
  • Core: A mover holding a fixed child stays sharp in the filter tier (b6f2bec16, #7292).
  • Core: The filter tier's svg no longer changes which child is last in the root (2066a2ff5, #7293).
  • Core: Blur quiets discrete callbacks instead of undoing what they wrote (cd7e93baa, #7271).

Internal

  • Engine: The effect scan resolves its options before scanning (ca64c2a6d, #7336).
  • Studio: Kill and retry a test Chrome that hangs after it starts listening (d2b170ce5, #7337).
  • Engine: Blur gates fail on CI when they cannot run, and prove blur drew (07824098e, #7302).

v1.4.253 Stable2026-11-07T10:47:35Z

A little update ✨

A small release with a few minor improvements. A bigger one is coming later 🫣

What's changed

  • Reduce repeated work during host-status updates by @nwparker in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/39340
  • Reduce process launches when reading Linux process start times by @OrcaWin in https://github.com/stablyai/orca/pull/39439

Full Changelog: https://github.com/stablyai/orca/compare/v1.4.252...v1.4.253

View release →

v1.4.252 Stable2026-11-07T04:03:24Z

Fork chats from any reply, Plan mode & a faster phone ✨

  • 📱 A smoother experience on your phone: The phone opens and reconnects sooner, scrolls and streams chats more smoothly, reads Markdown and math better, and keeps unsent chat, review and Tasks text across reconnects.
  • 🗂️ Organize workspaces across machines: Nest workspaces under a folder workspace, including workspaces on other machines.
  • 🌿 Do more with Chat UI: Fork any finished response to try another direction while the original chat stays as it was. In Claude or Codex chats, type /plan to switch to Plan mode, which persists when you reopen the chat.
  • 🖥️ A more reliable terminal: Terminal panes keep their zoom and restored scrollback, copied files paste as full paths, and busy terminal output takes less work to render.
  • 🤖 Better agent support: Open running subagents from chat, see proposed files and diffs in Codex approvals, answer OpenCode choice questions, and sign in to Antigravity Chat with Gemini Enterprise.
  • 🎙️ Better dictation: Pick your dictation language, keep the first and last words, see Listening while the model loads, and get on-device text back about a third sooner.
  • 🧭 Know where a chat runs: The chat composer shows the machine, workspace and branch, and chats show a card for each pull request the agent opens.
  • ⚡ Snappier desktop chats: One chat's status change no longer wakes every chat, the model picker opens without walking every worktree, and long replies stream without re-rendering.

Highlights

  • Chat: Fork from any finished response, use Plan mode in Claude and Codex chats, and open a running subagent from the agents strip. Codex approvals show the proposed files and diffs, OpenCode can ask choice questions, and Claude's built-in server tools show as normal tool cards. Holding Cmd/Ctrl in a busy chat shows what Cmd/Ctrl+Enter will do, app shortcuts work from the chat editor, and oversized pasted images shrink to fit instead of failing. Signed-out OpenCode, Pi and OMP chats offer a Sign in button.
  • Phone: Markdown is easier to read and renders math, GitHub alerts, task boxes and images. Rename a workspace from its menu, see automatic continue and Switch Account on usage-limit notices, and keep chat sends, PR text, review notes and Tasks drafts when a connection drops. The phone dials the relay right away when that worked last time, keeps terminal output across a network switch, and streams busy terminals without falling behind.
  • Agents & accounts: Antigravity Chat can sign in with Gemini Enterprise, reports quota for every saved account and manages accounts inside WSL. orca account can select and remove any saved account. Antigravity IDE joins the Open In presets.
  • Remote hosts: SSH says plainly when a host now serves a different Orca server, disconnects a host whose server you stopped, and honors IdentitiesOnly on manual hosts. Sleeping recipe VMs wake when any client opens them, orca serve can bind to one address, and browsing through a paired server is faster on high-latency links. Browser tabs show when an SSH host is controlling them, with a way to stop it.
  • Source control & Tasks: Phone branch switches are refused with a plain reason when they would move uncommitted work or land mid-rebase. Checks show when they ran and how long they took. Tasks can filter GitHub PRs and GitLab MRs by state and by who wrote or reviews them, and pasted images upload before a GitHub draft is published.
  • Workspaces & plugins: Multi-repo folder workspaces can parent workspaces, and workspaces can nest under parents on other machines. orca plugin installs, updates and removes plugins and manages catalogs, plugin pages show more about their publisher, and look-alike plugin names show who published them.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • macOS 13.4 minimum: Orca now needs macOS 13.4 or later (previously macOS 12). Macs on older versions stay on their last compatible build through the in-app updater and see a notice, and Homebrew refuses the upgrade on macOS 13.0–13.3. Any Mac on macOS 13 can update to macOS 13.7 to keep getting Orca updates.
  • Dictation model: The Zipformer EN dictation model is no longer offered. If you had it selected, Orca moves you to Parakeet v2, which you may need to download in Settings › Voice before dictation works.
  • New profiles: Fresh desktop profiles open in a folder workspace at your home folder, named ~. Existing profiles are unchanged. "Set up later" in onboarding now opens your default agent.
  • Pairing codes expire: Unused desktop pairing codes now expire: the phone QR code after 10 minutes and the pair-another-computer link after 60 minutes. Devices that are already paired are unaffected.
  • Chat UI settings: The changed files card and opened pull request cards are now controlled from Settings › Chat UI › Turn activity, as Turn diffs and Opened pull requests.
  • Local browser proxy: The local browser control proxy now requires a per-session token that only Orca's own agent-browser launches receive, so other programs on your machine can no longer use it.
  • Downgrading: Chats that used Plan mode can't be opened by v1.4.251 if you downgrade. They open again after you upgrade back.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v15.11.1 Stable2026-11-07T09:51:54Z

Pro sign-in works again

Signing in to cmem.ai Pro from the plugin returned a 500 error on every fresh plugin install. The plugin's install did not include better-auth, and its tables were never created. The plugin now ships better-auth and @better-auth/api-key as dependencies and creates the auth tables the first time they are needed (#5455, thanks @0xABCD01).

Fixes

  • Worker: when a run pauses after a transport, rate-limit or similar abort, it now releases its claims, so the paused session no longer holds pending work that can never expire (#5457, thanks @akashpandey).
  • Worker: an over-budget condense reply is clamped to the budget instead of being thrown away (#5452, thanks @xunguangzlj-cloud).
  • Worker: retry-without-reasoning now recognizes OpenCode Go's "cannot be represented" error wording (#5439, thanks @akashpandey).
  • Hooks: the Stop hook reads Claude Code's last_assistant_message directly and skips reading the transcript (#5449, thanks @xunguangzlj-cloud).
  • Context: the OAuth hint and gateway fallback notice are reserved before the context is fitted, so SessionStart context stays under Claude Code's 10,000-character limit (#5441, thanks @liuyinchu).
  • Codex: stale app-server child processes are recycled on the auth recovery probe (#5438, thanks @akashpandey).
  • Installer: the Goose YAML config is written atomically, so an interrupted install or uninstall can no longer leave a truncated config.yaml (#5442, thanks @DeryFerd).
  • Tests: the sync matrix waits for the page body before reading it, fixing a browser readiness race (#5448).

Also in this release

  • My Work: the scheduled refresh check interval is now a setting (#5437, thanks @weirui828).
  • Docs: one story across the README and docs: your coding agent forgets between sessions, Claude-Mem remembers (#5447).
  • Docs: README translations keep fenced code examples and single-quoted links intact (#5458, thanks @Afloat16).

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.11.0...v15.11.1

View release →

v15.11.0 Stable2026-11-06T22:17:48Z

Installer screen telemetry and runtime fixes

Claude-Mem now records progress through the local setup screens, so the admin can show where setup continues, goes back, encounters a known error, or has no recorded next step.

  • Track the nine actual setup screens: Scan, Reveal, Choose memory, Review savings, Checkout, Finish setup, Import history, Launch, and Workspace.
  • Count each rendered screen visit separately. Returning to a screen creates a new visit; failed hand-offs do not become successful forward transitions.
  • Preserve the real setup branches. At Home and existing Pro configurations skip checkout, history import returns to Finish setup, and reduced-motion setup skips the launch animation.
  • Record local setup completion only after the worker accepts the successful setup state write.
  • Preserve pending screen events when setup finishes and retry temporary local-worker connection failures, so a fast completion or a slow telemetry request does not lose the visit history.
  • Pass a fresh setup-attempt ID through the existing installer pairing when telemetry is enabled, allowing the matching admin report to connect setup progress to the authorized account flow.

The events follow the existing analytics settings, including DO_NOT_TRACK. Their schema accepts only known screen IDs, actions, error codes, timestamps, and random attempt/visit IDs. Prompts, filenames, URLs, credentials, and free-form error text are excluded. Sample previews do not emit setup events. The worker's long-lived anonymous identity is not aliased to an account.

Per-screen history starts with this release. Older installers did not record these screens, so their missing events are not treated as zero conversions or abandonment.

Runtime fixes included since v15.10.1

  • Stop replaying stale local observer work after quota or authentication recovery. Live capture uses a bounded handoff; pending inputs expire after five minutes and are discarded on quota/authentication failure. Saved memories and original transcripts remain intact. Capture skipped during an outage is not automatically backfilled.
  • Restore the server viewer's read endpoints using the server's Postgres data and existing team/project authorization. Shared server log files remain unavailable to tenant keys.
  • Stop advertising dumb_search on the server runtime, where only the local worker can execute it.
  • Derive the default worker port per installation to avoid local port collisions while preserving the legacy Windows server port.
  • Show files outside a project under the home directory as ~/… in generated context, rather than long chains of parent-directory segments.
  • Prefer the configured provider's agent for My Work refreshes.

Full comparison: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.10.1...v15.11.0

farion1231/cc-switch ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v4.0.27CC Switch v4.0.27 Stable2026-11-07T09:08:27Z

CC Switch v4.0.27

本版本让会话管理可以搜索消息正文，新增界面密度和界面缩放设置，模型定价表可以搜索，Grok Build 正在使用的供应商也能打开终端了。Skills 的已安装页重新排了版，供应商图标会跟随深浅色主题。另外修复了配置文件是软链接时被替换成普通文件或写入失败、取消请求后供应商卡在熔断恢复中直到重启、Codex 设置了自定义权限后 plan 模式报错、运行过的 Skill 被误报有更新、Skills 仓库显示 0 个 Skill、Windows 上把 Python 的 pi.exe 认成 Pi、模型价格同步超时、打开对话框时窗口拖不动、右键弹出浏览器菜单，以及 Linux 快捷面板显示异常等问题。4.0 完整的更新内容请见 v4.0 发布说明。

English → | 日本語版 →

新增

搜索会话的消息正文

以前会话管理页的搜索只能找标题、目录和会话 ID，记得聊过什么、却想不起是哪个会话时找不到（#7896、#7420）。

现在可以打开「搜索消息正文」：在页面右上角的「⋯」菜单里打开，或者搜索没有结果时直接点提示里的按钮。打开后，搜索结果会列出命中的正文片段和命中了几条消息。

  • 默认关闭。打开后 CC Switch 会在本地为消息正文建立索引，建立期间会显示进度，这时正文结果可能还不全。
  • 索引是单独的本地文件 session-index.db，不参与云同步，也不进备份；关闭这个开关会删除它。

界面密度和界面缩放

「设置 → 通用 → 外观」里新增两项（#9209）：

  • 界面密度：「紧凑」就是原来的尺寸，也是默认值；「舒适」的字号大一档，按钮和输入框更高一些。
  • 界面缩放：90%、100%、110%、125% 四档，也可以按 Ctrl（macOS 上是 ⌘）加 +、- 调整，按 Ctrl/⌘ 加 0 复原。

两项设置只保存在本机，和主题一样。托盘快捷面板始终保持紧凑尺寸。

模型定价表可以搜索

模型定价表上方加了搜索框，按模型 ID 或名称筛选（#9205）。按回车会编辑第一个结果；没有匹配的模型时，回车会为输入的模型新增定价。

Grok Build 打开终端

Grok Build 的供应商卡片也有「打开终端」按钮了（#9234）。Grok CLI 不能只让某一个终端换供应商，所以这个按钮只出现在正在使用的供应商上，打开的终端直接用 CC Switch 管理的这份 Grok 配置；选择跳过权限确认时会加上 --always-approve。

变更

  • Skills 已安装页重新排版（#9210）：
    • 名称列固定在左侧，窗口窄时各应用的列在它下面横向滚动。
    • 状态和来源筛选移到和「发现」页一样的工具栏里，选中 Skill 后也不会被挡住；批量操作保留更新、卸载和按应用「启用到」/「停用」。
    • 去掉了页头的「N 个可更新」链接，工具栏的「全部更新」已经写了数量；点每行的「可更新」标记就能更新这个 Skill。
    • 每行的「…」菜单保留打开文档、打开目录和卸载；写入失败时，错误信息显示在描述的位置。
  • 供应商图标跟随主题（#8438）：去掉了卡片图标后面固定的白底。单色的深色图标会跟着文字颜色变化，深色模式下也看得清；带深色笔画的多色图标、图片图标和你上传的图标在深色模式下仍然带浅色底。
  • 右键不再弹出浏览器菜单（#8919、#8921）：以前在主窗口和托盘面板里点右键，会弹出浏览器自带的菜单（Windows 上是「后退」「重新加载」「另存为」「打印」等）。现在不会了；输入框、代码编辑器和选中的文字仍然可以右键复制粘贴。
  • 熔断日志写明是哪家供应商（#9292）：熔断器的日志（CB-001 到 CB-006）都带上了「应用:供应商 id」；熔断器拒绝某家供应商时也会记一行 FWD-004，写明供应商名称，不再悄悄跳过。只改了日志，路由和熔断行为不变。
  • 预设： Mistral AI 预设的默认模型改为 mistral-large-4（Mistral Medium 3.5 已被 Mistral 宣布弃用），Claude Code、Codex 和 Claude Desktop 的 Mistral AI 预设不再隐藏。Mistral Vibe 套餐和 API 共用额度，「获取 API Key」改为打开 Vibe CLI 的密钥页，免费、订阅和按量付费都能用；添加供应商时搜「Vibe」也能找到它。新增 Genesis（#9269）、TokenPlus（#9296）。已添加的供应商不受影响。

修复

  • 配置文件是软链接时，切换供应商会把它替换成普通文件，Windows 上直接报错（#6764、#9283）。用 dotfiles 工具或同步文件夹管理 ~/.claude/settings.json、~/.codex/config.toml、opencode.json 等文件时，它们通常是软链接。以前写入时会把链接本身替换掉，macOS 和 Linux 上链接变成普通文件、和原文件脱钩，Windows 上报错「原子替换失败」（os error 1464）。现在会写到链接指向的真实文件，链接保持不变。
  • 请求中途被取消后，供应商可能一直处在熔断恢复中，直到重启（#7206、#9292）。熔断恢复期间，熔断器只放一个探测请求过去；如果这个请求还没结束客户端就断开了，名额不会还回去，之后发给这家供应商的请求全被拒绝。现在无论请求怎么结束，名额都会还回去。
  • Codex 设置了自定义权限后，用 plan 模式报错（#7349、#7294）。较新的 Codex 读取自定义权限时要求 model_catalog_json 是绝对路径，以前 CC Switch 写的是文件名，Codex 报 AbsolutePathBuf deserialized without a base path。现在写成绝对路径；配置目录在 WSL 里时仍写文件名。
  • 运行过一次的 Skill 被显示为「可更新」，点更新会覆盖整个目录（#7780、#7772）。Python 运行时生成的 __pycache__ 和 .pyc 文件被算进了 Skill 的内容。现在检查更新时忽略这些文件；以前版本记下的旧记录会自动换算，不会因此误报。
  • Skills 仓库显示「识别到 0 个技能」（#9273）。导入时添加的仓库分支记为 HEAD，或者仓库默认分支是 master 却配成了 main 时，数量总是 0。现在按实际读取的分支计数；读取失败的仓库会写明「读取失败」和原因，导入 Skill 新增了仓库后，下次打开会重新读取。
  • Windows 上把 Python 包里的 pi.exe 当成 Pi（#9267）。PyPI 上有一个无关的 pi 包会装出 Scripts\pi.exe，应用页会把它显示为「无法运行」。Pi 只通过 npm 发布，现在 Python Scripts 目录里的 pi.exe 不再被识别为 Pi。
  • 模型价格同步失败，只提示 error decoding response body（#9262）。网络较慢或经代理时，从 models.dev 下载价格表会在读到一半时超时。现在超时时间从 15 秒放宽到 60 秒，失败时同步状态里会写出真正的原因。
  • 添加 DeepSeek Harness 供应商时只提示 Invalid DeepSeek Harness profile YAML，不知道是哪个文件（#9297）。现在报错会写出出错的配置文件路径和 YAML 的具体错误，方便找到文件修复。
  • 连通性测试把会重定向的中转站判为不通（#9253、#5747）。有的网关在 /v1 和 /v1/ 之间互相重定向，现在测试收到重定向就算连得上。
  • 打开对话框时窗口拖不动（#9140）。现在对话框打开时仍然可以拖动窗口；Windows 和 Linux 上使用应用内窗口按钮时，最小化、最大化和关闭按钮也照常可用。
  • Linux 快捷面板显示异常：轻量模式下重新打开的面板和窗口渲染出错（#9233、#9216），设置了显示缩放时面板被裁掉一部分（#9287、#8556）。Linux 上的面板改为不透明背景。
  • 供应商名称以 emoji 等字符开头、或前面带空格时，没有图标的卡片显示的首字是乱码或空白（#8139）。
  • 一键导入链接的风险提示漏判了内嵌 IPv4 地址的 IPv6 地址（#8127）。这类地址现在按它实际指向的 IPv4 地址判断是否为本机或内网。

致谢

感谢 @funkpopo（界面密度和缩放、模型定价表搜索、Grok Build 打开终端、Skills 已安装页、对话框拖动、重定向中转站）、@Owlbay（消息正文搜索、供应商图标跟随主题、Linux 快捷面板）、@xue-ding-e（消息正文搜索）、@allenxu09（供应商图标跟随主题）、@SailingLoong（Codex plan 模式报错）、@rollroyces（Skill 误报有更新）、@jzcangshu（右键浏览器菜单）、@Railgun-wiki（Linux 快捷面板缩放）、@subbareddypalagiri（图标首字、一键导入风险提示）、@lxw665（Genesis 预设）、@XiaoqiangPei（TokenPlus 预设）的贡献。

Wei-Shaw/sub2api ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.2.20Sub2API 0.2.20 Stable2026-11-07T06:31:24Z

AI API Gateway Platform - 将 AI 订阅配额分发和管理

新增基于 OAuth 授权的管理 MCP 接口，并完善管理员密钥的细粒度权限；同时全面治理网关请求体内存与读写超时，提升高并发下的稳定性。

新增功能

  • 管理 MCP：新增 OAuth 认证的管理 MCP 接口及前端授权确认/连接管理页面，需显式配置 MCP_PUBLIC_URL 才会启用（默认关闭）
  • 管理员密钥：补全权限项（统计、OpenAI OAuth、邀请等）与中英文权限展示，新增需输入确认文字才能开启的「全权兼容模式」
  • 注册邮箱精确黑名单：支持批量粘贴（上限 20000 条），按收件箱身份匹配 Gmail 点号/+后缀变体，保存时可批量禁用命中的已注册用户
  • OpenAI 生图账号支持按 1K/2K/4K 尺寸档位分流，未指定尺寸时按档位偏好逐层选号
  • 新增 gateway.disable_openai_transport_error_cooldown 开关，可关闭 OpenAI 传输错误冷却
  • Antigravity 账号用量显示每个配额组真实的 5 小时与每周窗口，Pro 套餐的独立 Argon 配额单独成行
  • Antigravity INTERNAL 500 账号惩罚档位可通过环境变量配置

优化改进

  • 网关请求体内存预算、上游/下游读写超时与后台任务全面加上限，减少慢连接和大请求对实例的影响
  • 网关热路径减少 JSON 拷贝与重复解析（thinking 过滤、Codex 引导/压缩判断、Gemini 流增量等），RPM 覆盖与分组隐私门控不再每请求查库
  • 请求体读取失败按超时（408）、客户端断开（499）、截断、解码错误分类返回，并记录不含内容的诊断日志
  • 打通 GPT Image 2.6 原生图片链路，保留 complexity 与原生编辑选项
  • Antigravity 连接测试改为发送管理员填写的提示词并返回模型真实回复
  • Kimi 并发上限 403 改用短冷却，不再长时间下线账号
  • Antigravity 图像模型的 INTERNAL 500 只冷却该模型，不再连带整个账号进入惩罚
  • 用户使用记录导出每页 1000 条，遇面板限流 429 按 Retry-After 等待后重试
  • Codex 密钥导出与 CC Switch 导出默认模型统一改为 GPT-6.1 Sol，并启用实时联网搜索、保留图片配置
  • GPT-6.1 Terra 使用官方 Codex 描述（上下文窗口 500k），校验 effort 与兼容缓存键
  • 错误透传规则可覆盖内置的请求体过大 413
  • MCP 客户端 ID 过长时前端截断显示，MCP 关闭时隐藏入口

Bug 修复

  • 修复 Anthropic setup-token 账号在无 5h 限制时丢失每周用量的问题
  • 修复长会话中 cyber transcript 误拦截的问题（#8519）
  • 修复异步生图任务因请求体内存预算被提前释放而全部 503 的问题
  • 修复 Bedrock EventStream 帧长度未校验即分配内存的问题
  • 修复 Gemini 上游 SSE 行与 Antigravity 非流式响应读取无上限的问题
  • 修复补丁预算在 failover 中累积导致误拒绝的问题
  • 修复 OpenAI OAuth 透传对 codex 模型空 instructions 本地返回 403 的问题，response.instructions 回显按客户端原值还原
  • 修复非流式 /v1/responses 在上游返回 incomplete/cancelled 或缺少 Content-Type 的 SSE 时未返回 JSON 的问题
  • 修复 WebSocket HTTP 桥接未应用 Codex 指纹收敛的问题
  • 修复 OpenAI WS 透传部分轮次未记录首 token 时间的问题
  • 修复强制路由模式下仍发起 responses 探测的问题
  • 修复生图尺寸溢出选号时错误改写会话粘性绑定的问题
  • 修复 GPT Image 账号生图测试参数错误的问题
  • 修复 API Key 校验时客户端断开或密钥格式错误被报告为 500 的问题
  • 修复 Grok Voice 请求体读取失败被误报为 400 并回显原始 socket 错误的问题
  • 修复 Grok 委派消息输入在 Responses 中未规范化的问题
  • 修复 apicompat 转换丢失缓冲 reasoning 内容、完成响应丢失消息内容字段的问题
  • 修复 Gemini 原生 function response 中工具媒体丢失的问题
  • 修复 Antigravity 直连 Gemini 流式响应异常、responseJsonSchema 未改写为 responseSchema 的问题
  • 修复 Antigravity 转发时残留旧系统身份、误删携带任务内容的身份前导的问题
  • 修复 Cloudflare REST messages 请求携带 beta 查询参数的问题

破坏性变更

  • 请求体内存预算：新增 security.request_body_memory_budget_bytes（默认 1GiB），超出时返回 503 + Retry-After；设为 0 可禁用
  • 读写超时：新增 security.request_body_read_timeout_seconds 与 downstream_write_timeout_seconds（默认均 120 秒，0 禁用），上传单次读取停滞超时返回 408
  • 客户端断开后的上游 drain 窗口改为有上限，超时截断会影响该请求的计费口径；会话缓存改为有界
  • GPT-6.1 Terra：OAuth/setup-token 订阅请求超过 272k 不再收取长上下文附加费；Codex 窗口升至 500k 后 API Key 请求越过 272k 更频繁，按 2x/1.5x 计费；Terra 兼容路径的 minimal effort 改为返回 400
  • 新增数据库迁移 249_mcp_oauth.sql，升级时自动执行

升级指南

  • 容器内存较小或并发长上下文请求较多的部署，请按实际情况调整 security.request_body_memory_budget_bytes，详见 deploy/EDGE_SECURITY.md
  • 如需启用管理 MCP，配置 MCP_PUBLIC_URL（需 HTTPS，路径为 /api/v1/mcp）

📥 Installation

Docker:

# Docker Hub
docker pull weishaw/sub2api:0.2.20

# GitHub Container Registry
docker pull ghcr.io/wei-shaw/sub2api:0.2.20

One-line install (Linux):

curl -sSL https://raw.githubusercontent.com/Wei-Shaw/sub2api/main/deploy/install.sh | sudo bash

Manual download: Download the appropriate archive for your platform from the assets below.

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-07T05:17:04Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2042+g74f3ce7970
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1793383622

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.18.0 Stable2026-11-07T01:49:16Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Fixed

  • Fixed Mistral discovery listing embedding, OCR, moderation, transcription, and speech models as chat models; only rows with capabilities.completion_chat are offered, with context, image input, and reasoning read from the row (#18244 by @VeigaPunk)
  • A failed refresh no longer replaces a cached catalog whose models carry request headers with an empty snapshot; the next launch refetches it instead (#18227)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Fixed

  • Fixed transcript output freezing after provider restarts or switching sessions mid-stream (#18358 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Bash tool cards for a command whose leading cd <dir> && / cd <dir>; was hoisted into the working directory show that cd again above the visualized command, with the separator the model wrote, instead of dropping where it ran
  • Bash commands starting with cd <dir>; ... now run in <dir> as their working directory when it exists, so its direnv environment loads and the directory shows on the tool card (#18329 by @harshaygadekar)
  • Task-file approvals show and keep the checked file target through dispatch, so retargeted symlinks cannot silently substitute another assignment; replaced files require a retry (#18314 by @iliaal).
  • Subagents receive task instructions with their blank lines, Markdown hard breaks, and table spacing intact, whether given inline or via taskFile.
  • Hindsight retain and learn memories are no longer lost when delivery fails or omp exits: they are kept on disk, retried, and delivered by the next session without duplicates (#18176 by @alphastorm)
  • Fixed the session crashing with Native shell returned an invalid execution trace when a bash command assigned a long value containing multi-byte characters such as Chinese text (#18259)
  • grep -e and rg -e in the bash tool accept patterns that start with - (e.g. grep -e "--canary") instead of failing with an unknown-option error.
  • The bash execution visualization keeps updating, and saved traces still restore, after a command handles bytes that aren't valid UTF-8 (e.g. v=$(printf '\377'))
  • On Windows, grep, glob and isolated subagents no longer flash a console window when they query or start the ovnfs daemon.
  • A bash command containing a bare [ (e.g. a && [ while still streaming) no longer crashes omp in the Pseudo shell visualization (#18320)
  • Fixed a prompt that mentions an internal-scheme URL with @ (e.g. @local://x, @vault://x) aborting the run with "uses internal scheme ... not as a filesystem path"; such a mention is now left as plain text (#16071)
  • Fixed MCP OAuth login and token refresh failing with JSON Parse error: Unexpected identifier "access_token" against GitHub's token endpoint (#18292)
  • MCP OAuth requests tokens for the MCP server's own resource (from its protected-resource metadata) instead of a different resource advertised by the authorization server, fixing 401 invalid_token after /mcp reauth on servers such as Firecrawl's hosted MCP (#18315)
  • Concurrent subagent MCP calls now grant newly loaded tools to the session that made each call, even when providers reuse call IDs. (#18324 by @eggpeat)
  • LSP document symbols now show identifier lines, so those lines work with references, definition, and rename (#18257).
  • Subagents on worker threads no longer fail with Cannot send … instance across threads when their model comes from an extension-registered provider with its own streamSimple (#18310 by @Dangerboi)
  • ⌘V in terminals with kitty enhanced paste (OSC 5522, e.g. Ghostty 1.4) now attaches macOS TIFF screenshots instead of reporting "Clipboard paste has no supported text or image data" (#17696, #18203 by @roboomp)
  • Disabling display.shellVisualization now omits bash execution traces from tool updates and saved sessions, including in ACP (#18267).
  • /quit and /exit no longer wait several seconds for an unresponsive OTLP telemetry collector (#18255).
  • Cancelling a Python eval cell right as it starts now stops the cell cleanly instead of letting it run and killing the kernel (#18242 by @eggpeat)
  • Browser open now returns the same tab with a recovery notice when Chrome detaches the relay during navigation and omp successfully reattaches, instead of failing the open and closing newly opened tabs (#18252 by @will-bogusz)
  • tab.errors() keeps the fields of plain-object and other non-Error rejections, bounded like console arguments, instead of reporting only Object (#16959) (#18251 by @Alex-Wengg)
  • An extension provider with authHeader: true keeps its cached fetchDynamicModels catalog when omp models refresh fails, instead of showing 0 models until the next successful forced refresh (#18227)
  • A session whose request carries no titleable text of its own — an attached @brief.md, a greeting, a bare number — is now named once from the assistant's reading of it, instead of staying untitled until the next message that happens to be titleable (which, for an agent launched from one brief and left to run, never came) (#18238 by @sjawhar)
  • Prewalk no longer hands a subagent to its cheap model when the subagent messages its parent or another agent (write agent://…) before its first file edit (#18322 by @1vivy)

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Fixed

  • Fixed embedding rebuilds failing on hosted APIs that cap the inputs per request: embed() now splits large lists into requests of at most MNEMOPI_EMBEDDING_BATCH_SIZE texts (default 64; set 10 for DashScope) or the embeddings.batchSize runtime option, and returns null when the API returns a different number of vectors than texts (#18278 by @aleister1102)
  • Fixed an embedding model change deleting every stored vector when the new provider had no key, did not answer, or rejected a batch partway through: the store now embeds the whole corpus with the new model before replacing anything, keeps the old vectors if any request fails, and tries again on the next open (#18278 by @aleister1102)
  • Fixed rejected embedding API requests leaving no trace: the status and the first 300 characters of the response body now go to the log at debug, or at warn when runtime debug is on (#18278 by @aleister1102)

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Fixed

  • Fixed find() missing elements more than 24 levels down, such as VS Code's editor; it now searches 64 levels deep (#18221 by @will-bogusz)
  • glob, grep, fuzzyFind, ast_grep and ast_edit now run their directory traversal on a filesystem runtime of their own, so a search over a large or cold tree can no longer take the worker threads that the embedded shell and every other native call are scheduled on. Those threads are named pi-fs-worker, so a stall in a search is tellable apart from a stall elsewhere.
  • Fixed a workspace tree whose native addon does not match its checkout failing with undefined is not a constructor at the callsite: a class the addon lacks now throws a message naming the addon file, both releases and bun run build:native — from its constructor and from any static entrypoint — and the loader reports that mismatch once as it loads (#18230 by @sjawhar)

@oh-my-pi/pi-tui

Fixed

  • Ctrl+J from a win32-input-mode record with VK_J and Uc=LF now decodes as ctrl+j, so Windows Terminal / mini-term insert a newline instead of submitting (#18253 by @Clannad47)
  • The BTW history page (f on a side answer) scrolls its reader and history list with the mouse wheel, matching the other scrolling sheets (#18303 by @ImAlexBlock)
  • The BTW history list shows the date for questions asked before today (and the year for ones from an earlier year), instead of a bare time that read as today's; a follow-up asked on a later day than its topic shows its date too (#18210 by @a-glapinski)

@oh-my-pi/pi-utils

Fixed

  • Fixed abort signals leaving command output reads blocked when a descendant keeps stdout or stderr open after the root process exits. (#18309 by @eggpeat)

@oh-my-pi/pi-wire

Changed

  • ShellTrace fields that carry values from the run (argv, assignments, redirect targets, unlocated unit sources, diagnostics) are ShellTraceBytes: a string when the bytes are UTF-8, otherwise the raw byte values. isShellTrace accepts both forms

What's Changed

  • fix(builtins): accept dash-leading patterns after grep/rg -e by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18287
  • fix(shell): cut assign previews on a utf-8 character boundary by @roboomp in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18265
  • fix(ovnfs): hide the console window when querying or starting the daemon by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18290
  • obash: let the OS do teardown, where applicable by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18289
  • fix(wire): accept shell trace byte values that are not UTF-8 by @H4vC in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18291
  • fix(hindsight): persist tool retains in a durable outbox by @alphastorm in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18176
  • fix(browser): keep the tab an open successfully reattaches by @will-bogusz in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18252
  • fix(lsp): used identifier lines for document symbols by @roboomp in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18261
  • fix(mnemopi): split embedding requests and keep vectors on failed model switch by @aleister1102 in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18278
  • fix(mcp): requested json from oauth token endpoints by @roboomp in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18293

  • fix(utils): retain abort handling until output collection finishes by @eggpeat in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18309
  • docs(task): state the subagent worker threshold the code uses by @Dangerboi in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18311

  • fix(prewalk): don't hand off on file-free coordination writes by @1vivy in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18322

  • fix(natives): name the stale addon when a class it predates is constructed by @sjawhar in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18230
  • fix(session): title a session whose request carries no titleable text by @sjawhar in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18238
  • fix(eval): preserve Python interrupts before cell startup by @eggpeat in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18242
  • fix(catalog): limit Mistral discovery to chat-capable models by @VeigaPunk in https://github.com/can1357/oh-my-pi/pull/18244

Fission-AI/OpenSpec ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.16.1v1.16.1 - Spec parsing, version checks Stable2026-11-07T00:44:30Z

What's New in v1.16.1

A patch release that makes spec validation stricter and more forgiving in the right places, and makes openspec version --check reliable.

Fixed

  • Closed section headings - validate, show, list, view and archive now read headings with a closing # run (## Purpose ##, ## ADDED Requirements ##) the same as their unclosed forms.
  • Unrecognized delta sections - validate now fails, and archive refuses, when a delta spec puts a requirement under an unrecognized section such as ## DEPRECATED Requirements, instead of passing with a warning and silently dropping that requirement.
  • Version check with telemetry off - openspec version --check now checks for a newer version even when telemetry is off or it runs in CI. Only OPENSPEC_NO_UPDATE_CHECK skips it; the background check in openspec update still honors the telemetry opt-out and CI.
  • Standalone update offers - openspec version --check on the standalone binary now offers only a release whose files are attached on GitHub, the same one openspec install would install, rather than a version npm has before the installer can get it.

Full Changelog: https://github.com/Fission-AI/OpenSpec/compare/v1.16.0...v1.16.1

harry0703/MoneyPrinterTurbo ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.3.10 Stable2026-11-07T00:43:54Z

English | 中文

<h2 id="en-v1.3.10">Highlights</h2>

  • Added OpenAI Codex subscription access with device-code sign-in and dynamic model selection, plus a separate ChatGPT Native OAuth provider. Native OAuth availability depends on account eligibility; existing Codex sign-in remains independent.
  • Added Codex image generation as a material source. Text and image generation can share the application's Codex login, and generated images support the existing static and slow zoom effects.
  • Added Infistar and Jalapeno Cloud for LLM and video generation, alongside Runcrate, CloudGPU, and cheapaimodel LLM providers.
  • Improved long-running video generation feedback with generation stages, elapsed time, and polling status. Task tracking survives page refreshes and temporary query failures, with protection against duplicate submissions.
  • Simplified provider settings and selection in the WebUI, making configured services easier to find and switch between.
  • Added Arabic-script subtitle shaping, including connected letters, right-to-left layout, mixed-script font fallback, and glyph-aware wrapping and backgrounds.
  • Made slow image zoom smoother with sub-pixel rendering, eliminating visible stepping and wobble.
  • Added Indonesian to script-language choices and refined the Indonesian interface translations.

Fixes and Improvements

  • Fixed long Edge TTS narration timing out because of unnecessary delays between streamed chunks. Added support for Edge voices with script subtags and clearer Gemini empty-audio diagnostics.
  • Removed emoji from narration and burned-in subtitles, and clarified that Gemini and MiMo TTS do not use the voice-speed setting.
  • Improved subtitle splitting around clock times, full-width numbers, dates, abbreviations, names, and Chinese and Japanese punctuation. Added sentence splitting for Thai and Lao scripts.
  • Preserved the original timed transcription when subtitle correction cannot align the full script. Fixed empty-string and Unicode length-expansion cases in subtitle similarity scoring.
  • Preserved combining marks in multilingual social hashtags, excluded emoji marks, and recognized Chinese and Japanese hashtag separators.
  • Kept existing script and keywords when AI generation fails, displayed an actionable error, and avoided requesting keywords after script generation has already failed.
  • Preserved the position of repeated topics during script-order stock-material matching, repaired corrupt video caches, and added clearer Pexels and Coverr search-failure logs.
  • Stopped console keystrokes from prematurely ending or stalling FFmpeg clip concatenation. Fixed render-output synchronization on Windows and big-endian grayscale TIFF brightness.
  • Improved Windows startup from paths containing special characters, from other working directories, and with LAN listening enabled. Fixed redirected console logs and preserved Windows paths and non-breaking spaces when saving configuration.
  • Shared Redis task-concurrency limits across workers, with atomic slot reservation, lease renewal, and queued-task recovery.
  • Improved subtitle-font error messages, API enum defaults, and ChatGPT/Codex sign-in diagnostics. Clarified Docker configuration setup and recovery from an accidentally mounted config.toml directory.
  • Updated CPU-container dependencies to address fixable security findings and added container vulnerability scanning. Expanded regression coverage and corrected Windows-specific path assertions.

<h2 id="cn-v1.3.10">重点更新</h2>

  • 新增 OpenAI Codex 订阅接入，支持设备码登录和动态模型选择；另提供独立的 ChatGPT 原生 OAuth 接入。原生授权取决于账号资格，与已有 Codex 登录相互独立。
  • 新增 Codex 图片素材来源，文案与生图可复用本应用的 Codex 登录，生成的图片支持现有的静止和缓慢放大效果。
  • 新增 Infistar 和 Jalapeno Cloud 大模型及视频生成服务，以及 Runcrate、CloudGPU、cheapaimodel 大模型服务。
  • 完善长时间视频生成的进度反馈，显示生成阶段、已用时间和轮询状态；刷新页面或查询短暂失败后可继续跟踪任务，并增加重复提交保护。
  • 简化 WebUI 的服务商设置与选择，便于查找和切换已经配置的服务。
  • 新增阿拉伯语文字字幕整形，支持字母连写、从右到左排版、混合文字字体回退，以及按实际字形计算换行和背景。
  • 改善图片缓慢放大的流畅度，通过亚像素渲染消除明显的步进和抖动。
  • 新增印尼语脚本语言选项，并完善印尼语界面翻译。

修复与改进

  • 修复 Edge TTS 因流式数据处理延迟导致长文案配音超时的问题；支持带脚本子标签的 Edge 音色，并补充 Gemini 返回空音频时的原因提示。
  • 从配音和烧录字幕中清理 emoji，明确提示 Gemini、MiMo TTS 不使用配音语速设置。
  • 改善时间、全角数字、日期、缩写、人名及中日文标点附近的字幕断句；补充泰语、老挝语按句间空格拆分的处理。
  • 字幕校正无法对齐完整文案时保留原始转写时间轴；修复相似度计算遇到空字符串或特殊 Unicode 字符时除零、返回负值的问题。
  • 保留多语言社交标签中的组合字符，排除 emoji 标记，并识别中文、日文标签分隔符。
  • AI 文案或关键词生成失败时保留已有内容并显示错误；文案生成失败后不再继续请求关键词。
  • 修复按脚本顺序匹配库存素材时重复主题丢失位置的问题；修复损坏的视频缓存，并补充 Pexels、Coverr 搜索失败的具体原因。
  • 避免控制台按键提前结束或阻塞 FFmpeg 片段拼接；修复 Windows 渲染文件同步，以及大端灰度 TIFF 图片亮度问题。
  • 改善 Windows 下特殊字符路径、其他工作目录和局域网监听场景中的启动兼容性；修复日志重定向，并在保存配置时保留 Windows 路径和不换行空格。
  • 统一多个工作进程的 Redis 任务并发限制，支持原子占用执行名额、续期和排队任务恢复。
  • 改善字幕字体错误提示、API 枚举默认值和 ChatGPT/Codex 登录诊断；补充 Docker 配置说明，以及误将 config.toml 挂载为目录时的恢复提示。
  • 更新 CPU 容器依赖，修复可解决的安全扫描问题，并增加容器漏洞扫描；完善回归测试，修正 Windows 路径断言误报。
